# #1073 — OmniRoute July 16–31 per-PR dispositions

Reviewed 2026-11-20 against the current DurinDoor checkout. Scope is the inclusive UTC merged-date window **2026-07-16 through 2026-07-31** in the retained public source metadata at `/tmp/durindoor-omni-july-mg7fnibh`. This record covers this date shard only.

**Inventory:** 840 unique PR IDs; merged-date membership checked for every row. Each source link points to the PR and each commit is the exact source merge commit recorded in metadata. Exact changed paths are listed per row where complete. The pinned metadata has complete changed-path lists for 830 rows. Ten rows are truncated at 100 returned paths: the unavailable remainder totals 3822 paths; those rows remain unresolved and no classifications are extrapolated from partial lists.

Dispositions use conservative evidence. “Upstream-only docs/tests/CI/release” means every available path is confined to that surface. “Provider Sign-in” is used only when the full path list explicitly adds provider sign-in/credential or provider-registry surfaces. Other functional changes remain unresolved unless a listed local comparison supports a narrower acceptance. Path names and titles do not establish behavior.

## Previously focused July examples

- #6937 was already discussed in `1073-historical-coverage-reconciled.md` as an anchored translator source with a port commit not in current ancestry. Its Grok Responses tool-call shape is **unresolved** here: local `open-sse/translator/response/openai-responses.js` has custom-tool input event handling, but that does not establish equivalence to this Grok-specific change. It is not counted as a newly confirmed gap.
- #6794 and #7210 are independently checked below. #7210 is already present for its narrow Codex plan-label behavior.

## Deep static checks of representative unresolved existing-surface rows

- **#6937:** local Responses translator handles `custom_tool_call` item and input delta/done events (`open-sse/translator/response/openai-responses.js`); source metadata identifies only the Responses translator and a focused test. Grok-specific alignment is **unresolved** without establishing the exact final-source behavior.
- **#7545:** local combo response-quality handling is routed through `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.js` and quality helpers; source metadata identifies combo validation plus a targeted test. Mid-lifecycle truncated-SSE failover equivalence is **unresolved**; presence of a quality module alone does not prove failover semantics.
- **#7779:** local request processing applies compression through `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.js` and RTK helpers. This does not establish whether routing-combo assignments are consumed by the local combo factory; disposition **unresolved**.
- **#6794:** source concerns Electron standalone packaging. The local tree contains `scripts/build/` tooling but source metadata alone plus similarly named packaging scripts does not establish the hashed-module symlink behavior; disposition **unresolved**.
- **#7210:** local provider connection row and usage limits render Codex plan badges using `getCodexPlanLabel` / `getCodexPlan` (`src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/providers/[id]/ConnectionRow.js`, `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/usage/components/ProviderLimits/index.js`). Accept **already present for the narrow plan-label display**; this does not claim identical upstream implementation.
